The reversed chain in 92:8–10 then sharpened the inferred giving-to-protection arrow: it is a contrastive mechanism, not a causal statement supplied by the grammar.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_092dde4377621369bb81","text":"2. **Pliant compliance as the bodily mechanism of ease.** Evidence: `ع ط و` B006 (yielding/compliant ease), `و ق ي` B001 (protective barrier), activated by `ي س ر` B005 in 92:7. Structural cue: the sequence moves from yielding/giving to being made easy. Mechanism: a pliant will can be conducted along the easy route. Abductive move: I infer a feedback loop: one yields in giving, then is made more yieldingly mobile. Reading change: “gave” includes a posture of being led, not only a transfer of wealth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_16c5687a68f3f955288c","text":"92:20’s “face” was the decisive later activator: تولى became the exact opposite of seeking the Lord’s وجه. The ayah now reads as a refusal of available proximity, not merely physical retreat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4fa3fc3d2bf75eae5928","text":"3. **Overreaching blocked by taqwā.** Evidence: `ع ط و` B004 (taking on what one has no right to), `و ق ي` B002. Structural cue: 92:8 gives the opposite as withholding plus self-sufficiency. Mechanism: piety guards giving from becoming possessive overreach or status-performance. Abductive move: I supply the danger that giving can be contaminated by self-claim. Reading change: the ayah praises a giving that is protected from its own possible domination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7c64b0c2608fc4e6fc41","text":"2. **Release as pliant rather than forced transfer.** Alongside ordinary giving, `ع ط و:B006` supplies yielding, compliant ease. The nearby doubled `ي س ر` in 92:7 and the contrastive withholding of 92:8 activate that material image: one hand/motion yields, the other arrests flow. The mapping from a pliant bow or mount to human transfer is analogical and therefore exploratory; the packet does not state that ease causes giving. It nevertheless changes `أَعْطَىٰ` from a quantified loss into a posture of unresisting release that is then made easier to continue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B006","root_001677/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9ab861d20be9c644a2d4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:5:3\",\"root_001028\",[\"B006\"]],[\"92:5:4\",\"root_001677\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Vermedeki gönüllü esneme, kişiyi kolay yolda ilerlemeye elverişli kılan bedensel bir yumuşaklık gibi çalışır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9ac6facb28ddecd11ffa","text":"1. **Giving as the construction of protection.** `أَعْطَىٰ` anchors `ع ط و:B002` (handing over and giving), while `ٱتَّقَىٰ` anchors `و ق ي:B002` (placing the self in protective caution) and rests on `و ق ي:B001` (a barrier that wards off harm). The grammar coordinates giving before guarding; confirmation and facilitation follow in 92:6–7, whereas withholding begins the reverse sequence in 92:8–10. The packet supplies order and contrast; the inferred arrow is that releasing possession helps build the giver’s protective barrier. Giving therefore reads as an operative means of protection, not an act merely adjacent to piety.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aadc9a7d7d004db2c9e4","text":"1. **Giving as handover plus self-guarding.** Evidence: `ع ط و` B002 (handing over/giving), `و ق ي` B002 (placing self in protective caution). Structural cue: giving is paired with guarding before truth in 92:6 and ease in 92:7. Mechanism: release of possession and protective self-discipline form one movement.
